- **hlr-argument-structure**: Use when organizing the body of a Harvard Law Review (HLR) piece along the doctrine to theory to normative-prescription arc that legal scholarship rewards. - **hlr-footnotes-and-cite-check**: Use when building and stress-testing the heavy footnote apparatus of a Harvard Law Review (HLR) piece so it survives the student-editor cite-check / source-pull. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Harvard-Law-Review-Skills/skills/hlr-footnotes-and-cite-check) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **hlr-placement-strategy**: Use when planning where and when to submit a law-review article and how to leverage offers — the distinctive Scholastica multi-submit, expedite mechanics, and the February-March / August seasons, with the Harvard Law Review (HLR) as a top target. Plans placement and timing; it does not run the final upload preflight (hlr-submission). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Harvard-Law-Review-Skills/skills/hlr-placement-strategy) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **hlr-preemption-check**: Use when verifying that a Harvard Law Review (HLR) argument has not already been made — the law-review "preemption check" run across SSRN, Westlaw/Lexis, HeinOnline, and Google Scholar BEFORE drafting. - **hlr-revision-and-editing**: Use when working through the Harvard Law Review (HLR) post-acceptance editing cycle — successive substantive and technical edit rounds, responding to editor markups, and page proofs to publication. Drives the edit cycle to completion; it does not manage the offer/expedite phase (hlr-placement-strategy) or the first editor handshake (hlr-student-editor-review). Key handling (min L bytes, keys &gt; B hashed, random, periodic refresh), precomputation optimization (cache padded key hash state), truncation rules (min half hash output, min 80 bits). Security analysis (collision resistance with secret IV, birthday attack 2^(L/2) impractical, hash collision attacks don't break HMAC, transient effect). Test vectors. - **hmct-workflow**: HMCT 模型转换与 PTQ 量化总入口——对浮点 Caffe/ONNX，以及 PyTorch 等框架导出 ONNX 后的标准 PTQ，优先使用 hb_config_generator 生成官方 YAML，再运行 hb_compile -c。 根据用户意图自动路由： (1) 提供了校准数据 → 使用 hb_config_generator 生成官方 YAML 配置，再运行 hb_compile -c 执行标准 PTQ；PyTorch .pt/.pth 默认先检查导出受支持 ONNX 的条件； (2) 未提供校准数据 → 使用 hb_compile -m --march 检查模型； (3) 用户希望进行精度调优 → 转交 s-hmct-cosine-similarity-tuning SKILL 执行多阶段调优； (4) 用户希望进行单项精度 debug 分析（节点灵敏度、数据分布、累积误差等）→ 调用 hmct-debugger CLI 执行对应分析工具。 当用户提示词中出现 HMCT、模型转换、模型量化、PTQ、hb_compile、YAML 配置、精度调优、cosine similarity、节点灵敏度、数据分布、累积误差、debug 等关键词时应触发此 Skill。 ([D-Robotics/rdk-skills](https://github.com/D-Robotics/rdk-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/oe-skills-s/skills/hmct) · ★6)
- **hmda-analysis**: Pull and describe HMDA mortgage-lending data (LAR records) for a county, state, lender (LEI), or multiple years, and compute a CRA-PROXY borrower- and tract-income distribution. - **hmem-search**: Search hmem memory for things referenced without an ID. Do NOT trigger for explicit ID lookups like "read P0048". ([Bumblebiber/hmem](https://github.com/Bumblebiber/hmem/tree/HEAD/skills/hmem-search-workspace/skill-snapshot) · ★24)

- **hmm-based-cas-protein-search**: Use when raw paired-end genomic FASTQ reads (isolate or metagenome) should be scanned for cas genes/CRISPR arrays without a complete assembly, e.g. phage-therapy susceptibility screening or novel CRISPR/Cas discovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hmm-based-cas-protein-search))
- **hmm-based-copy-number-segmentation**: Use when the goal is to infer CNV/LOH events from raw RNA-seq signal profiles (bulk or single-cell) without a high-quality heterozygous SNV call set, e.g., tumor scRNA-seq clonal architecture questions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hmm-based-copy-number-segmentation))
- **hmm-based-enriched-region-detection**: Use when calling protein-DNA binding sites from ChIP-Seq BED or ELAND read files (single-end or paired-end), especially when reproducing HPeak peak counts or comparing peak callers on datasets such as STAT1, NRSF, H3K4me3, or H3K27me3. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hmm-based-enriched-region-detection))
- **hmm-based-genome-annotation**: Use when starting from nucleotide (or already gene-predicted) bacterial genome FASTAs and you need Pfam protein family presence/absence profiles as features for trait prediction or comparative genomics, following Traitar's Prodigal + HMMER/Pfam 27.0 workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hmm-based-genome-annotation))
- **hmm-based-methylation-scoring**: Use when basecalled Nanopore reads (FASTQ) with raw FAST5 signals must be scored for CpG methylation via an HMM, or when comparing GPU vs CPU (OpenMP) methylation-calling performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/hmm-based-methylation-scoring))
- **hmm-based-modification-enzyme-annotation**: Use when mining nucleotide genomes (FASTA contigs or assemblies) for RiPP-encoding BGCs and you need to locate modification enzyme homologs to define candidate precursor peptide regions before spectra matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hmm-based-modification-enzyme-annotation))
- **hmm-based-occupancy-modeling**: Use when you have paired-end MNase-seq and/or ATAC-seq data plus genome sequence and need probabilistic genome-wide occupancy profiles of nucleosomes and DNA-binding factors, especially to benchmark against NucleoATAC, DANPOS2, or MACS2+FIMO. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hmm-based-occupancy-modeling))
- **hmm-based-protein-classification**: Use when input is a protein sequence set in FASTA format and the goal is automatic detection and enzyme-type prediction of proteins belonging to known paralogous families, where blast-based or phylogenetic assignment is too slow or error-prone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hmm-based-protein-classification))
- **hmm-based-rrna-gene-identification**: Use when you need to detect SSU rRNA genes within large sequence collections (e.g. non-redundant SILVA releases or metagenome assemblies) so that structural features such as ≥10 bp insertion boundaries can be located from alignment gaps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hmm-based-rrna-gene-identification))
- **hmm-based-transcribed-region-prediction**: Use when scRNA-seq must be analyzed without a high-quality gene annotation: STAR- or CellRanger-aligned read alignments are available and genome-wide transcribed regions (TARs) must be predicted de novo from read coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hmm-based-transcribed-region-prediction))
- **hmm-cell-cycle-phase-segmentation**: Use when you have an ordered single-cell pseudo time-series (e.g., reCAT ordIndex from consensus-TSP) with Bayes/mean scores and need to assign each cell to a cell cycle stage such as G1, S, or G2/M. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hmm-cell-cycle-phase-segmentation))
- **hmm-coverage-profile-modeling**: Use when calling protein-DNA binding peaks from short-read ChIP-Seq data with explicit statistical modeling of sequencing noise, or when reproducing HPeak peak counts and motif-enrichment comparisons against other peak-callers on datasets like the NRSF ChIP-Seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hmm-coverage-profile-modeling))
- **hmm-differential-call-post-processing**: Use when you have histoneHMM differential calls for a broad histone mark (e.g. H3K9me3 female-vs-mouse male) and need to evaluate them against a defined gene set by base-pair and gene-count overlap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hmm-differential-call-post-processing))
- **hmm-hit-precision-sensitivity-calculation**: Use when KEMET --hmm_mode onebm HMM hits on MAGs must be validated against the same HMM search on corresponding NCBI/PATRIC complete isolate genomes, and precision/sensitivity of recovered missing KOs is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hmm-hit-precision-sensitivity-calculation))
- **hmm-hit-threshold-filtering**: Use when KEMET HMM-search (--hmm_mode onebm/module/kos) output must be filtered so only KOs whose HMM profile score passes the quality cutoff (default 0.43) count as recovered annotations, e.g. in truncation or KO-removal recovery tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hmm-hit-threshold-filtering))
- **hmm-initialization-from-summary-statistics**: Use when calling ChIP-Seq peaks with a two-state HMM over 25 bp HDF coverage bins and you need defensible starting values for transition and GP/ZIP emission parameters prior to Viterbi refinement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hmm-initialization-from-summary-statistics))
- **hmm-knn-cna-state-smoothing**: Use when calling per-gene, per-cell copy-number states from sparse, low-coverage scRNA-seq read-depth (RDR) or B-allele-frequency (BAF) matrices where raw signals are too noisy and need smoothing on gene-coordinate and cell-neighborhood graphs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hmm-knn-cna-state-smoothing))
- **hmm-posterior-decoding-forward-backward**: Use when you need probabilistic, per-genomic-region classifications of ChIP-seq enrichment for broad histone marks (e.g. H3K27me3, H3K9me3) rather than binary peak calls, in one- or two-sample comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hmm-posterior-decoding-forward-backward))
- **hmm-profile-alignment-scoring**: Use when benchmarking a plant R protein predictor against the HMMER profile alignment baseline, especially when negative training set sequences are annotated incompletely under HMMER so scoring must be restricted to positive sequences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/hmm-profile-alignment-scoring))
- **hmm-profile-annotation-against-pfam**: Use when starting from nucleotide FASTA genomes (or already-predicted ORFs) and you need Pfam-A protein family profiles as features for Traitar phenotype prediction across tens or hundreds of bacterial samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hmm-profile-annotation-against-pfam))
- **hmm-profile-database-construction**: Use when you need to rebuild or customize the biosynthetic pHMM reference library (feature set 1) that BiG-SLiCE uses to project BGCs into Euclidean feature space, e.g. after a Pfam release update. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/hmm-profile-database-construction))
- **hmm-profile-database-querying**: Use when when you have BGC sequences (FASTA or GenBank format) or protein sequences and need to annotate them with biosynthetic or functional domains from a curated pHMM database (e.g., PFAM 35.0). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/hmm-profile-database-querying))
- **hmm-profile-leave-one-out-validation**: Use when a curated HMM-based classifier (e.g. the DsrABCMK model set behind DiSCo) needs its stability tested against training-set composition, i.e. checking whether model assignments or type predictions change when single training sequences are dropped. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hmm-profile-leave-one-out-validation))
- **hmm-profile-ortholog-recovery**: Use when eggNOG-mapper/.emapper.annotations or similar KO annotations are suspected incomplete (e.g. fragmented MAG assemblies or truncated genes) and missing KOs from KEGG Modules need evidence-backed recovery via HMM profile search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hmm-profile-ortholog-recovery))
- **hmm-profile-protein-family-detection**: Use when building protein-family inventories or presence/absence matrices from predicted ORFs whose sequence divergence defeats keyword-based annotation — e.g. surveying ribosomal protein L30, L9, L1 absence across divergent lineages such as CPR and TM6. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hmm-profile-protein-family-detection))
- **hmm-profile-search-annotation-recovery**: Use when MAG or draft-genome eggNOG/KAAS/KofamKoala KO annotations leave KEGG Modules incomplete and unannotated genes (including partially truncated sequences) need evidence-based KO recovery via profile HMMs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hmm-profile-search-annotation-recovery))
- **hmm-profile-threshold-calibration**: Use when running KEMET's HMM-based missing-KEGG-ortholog search (--hmm_mode onebm) and you need to tune or justify the nhmmer score-per-length cutoff against verified reference hits rather than accepting the 0.43 default. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hmm-profile-threshold-calibration))
- **hmm-protein-homology-search-of-reads**: Use when raw, unassembled Illumina FASTQ reads must be screened for remote protein homologs (e.g. cas genes) and individual matching reads are needed as seeds, rather than assembled contigs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hmm-protein-homology-search-of-reads))

- **hmm-search-threshold-filtering**: Use when you need to identify and type-classify Dsr-dependent dissimilatory sulphur metabolism proteins from FASTA protein data using profile HMMs with model-specific score and E-value cut-offs rather than phylogenetic assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hmm-search-threshold-filtering))
- **hmm-transcribed-region-calling-from-sc-rna-seq**: Use when raw scRNA-seq FASTQs must be analyzed without relying on a complete gene annotation — e.g., to quantify reads falling outside GENCODE/refFlat gene bodies in less-studied organisms (lemur, mole rat, sea urchin). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hmm-transcribed-region-calling-from-sc-rna-seq))

- **hmmer-pfam-domain-detection**: Use when antiSMASH GenBank BGC outputs must be assigned conserved protein domains before BGC similarity networking (BiG-SCAPE), or when per-domain-type alignments are needed for GCF/clan clustering or CORASON-style analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/hmmer-pfam-domain-detection))
- **hmmer-profile-hmm-construction**: Use when candidate KO annotations are missing from a MAG/genome annotation table (eggNOG, KAAS, KOFAMkoala) and you want to recover them via profile HMM searches against taxonomically relevant KEGG GENES ortholog subsets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hmmer-profile-hmm-construction))
